- i=j: j≤n-k-1: g_{j+k}
- i+k=j => i=j-k: j≥k: -h_{j-k}

Block LP algorithm:
```
def block_lp_iteration(h, n, deadline, use_lp=True):
  best_h=h; best_c=compute_c5
  h = project_feasible(h)
  for iter in range(max_iters):
    if time deadline break
    q = 1 - h
    p = lp_min_max_p(q,n,deadline maybe)
    if p is None: break
    p = project_feasible(p)
    c = compute_c5(p,n)
    if c < best: update h=p
    else:
       # maybe blend with previous to avoid divergence
       pblend = 0.5*h + 0.5*p; project; if improves update
    qh = 1 - h  # next q
```

Let's derive a robust 1D minimizer for ≤m quadratics:

- Bound lo,hi.

- For each active q, if |b_q|>eps, vertex d=-a_q/(2b_q) if within (only relevant if b positive? include all).

Implement `c_fft(h,n)`:
```
N=1
M=2*n-1
while N<M: N*=2
r=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h,N)*np.conj(np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,N)), N, n=N)
L=n-1
c=np.empty(M)
c[L]=r[0]
if n>1:
  c[:L]=r[n-1:0:-1]
  c[L+1:]=r[N-1: N-n? Wait positive k 1..n-1: c[L+k]=r[N-k]. So c[L+1:] = r[N-1 down to N-(n-1)]? pos k=1->r[N-1]; k=n-1->r[N-n+1]. So c[L+1:] = r[N-1: N-n: -1]? For slice stop exclusive: r[N-1 : N-n : -1] gives indices N-1 down to N-n+1, length n-1. Good.
  c[:L] = r[n-1:0:-1] (indices n-1 down to1)
```

```
def project_feasible(x,n):
  x=np.asarray(x,float)
  S=n/2.0
  if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): x=np.full(n,0.5)
  lo=np.min(x)-1.0; hi=np.max(x)+1.0
  for _ in range(80):
    mid=(lo+hi)/2
    y=np.clip(x+mid,0,1)
    s=y.sum()
    if s < S: lo=mid
    else: hi=mid
  return np.clip(x+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```
This projects x, not x? For Euclidean projection onto box+sum, variable λ is added to x. Bisection finds λ. Sum enough. But if x has huge values, lo/hi broad; 80 bits enough double. Could be slow 80*n per call. n=1000, 80k; okay. Previous project loop 3000* maybe worse. Could use 60.
Need ensure if target S outside 0..n? S=n/2 okay. If n=0 no.

def c5_direct(h,n):

Now smooth L-BFGS implementation details:
```
def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
  c=c_full(x,n)
  m=c.max()
  sh=alpha*(c-m)
  mx=sh.max()
  logsum=mx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx)))
  val=m+logsum/alpha
  if mu>0: val += mu*(x.sum()-n/2.0)**2
  return float(val)

def smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,mu):
  c=c_full(x,n)
  m=c.max()
  sh=alpha*(c-m)
  mx=sh.max()
  w=np.exp(sh-mx); w/=w.sum()
  dx=2.0/n
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  # threshold weights > 1e-12 maybe to avoid loops over 2n
  idx=np.nonzero(w>1e-10)[0]
  for idx_val in idx:
    k=idx_val-(n-1)
    wm=w[idx_val]*dx
    if abs(wm)<1e-16: continue
    if k>=0:
      nv=n-k
      grad[:nv] += wm*(1.0-x[k:n])
      grad[k:n] += -wm*x[:nv]
    else: ...
  if mu: grad += 2*mu*(x.sum()-n/2)
  return grad
```
For `w > 1e-10`, sum weights omitted, gradient of logsum approximation maybe slight; okay. Could set threshold=1e-12. For low alpha, w distributed many (2n-1 up to 2047), loop 2000 per grad; for n=1000 okay? 2000*1000=2M per grad if all active; for 10k eval 20B Python loops too slow. Need active threshold depending alpha. For low alpha, we can instead compute full gradient via convolution? Or avoid low alpha? For low alpha, gradient average can be approximated? Let's design: If number active > max_active, select top max_active by weight. Since softmax for small alpha has many but we can approximate gradient by top weights or use numerical? For L-BFGS, gradient accuracy matters. But low alpha early can be skipped? Parent used alphas [50...] and looped over all active if w>1e-14; for n=500, 999 loops each grad maybe okay in Python? 999*500=500k per grad, 10k=5e9 Python-level slice ops? ```
def active_c_vec(x,n,active):
  c=np.empty(len(active))
  for t,idx in enumerate(active):
    k=idx-(n-1)
    if k>=0: nv=n-k; c[t]=np.dot(x[0:nv], 1-x[k:n])
    else: a=-k; c[t]=np.dot(x[a:n], 1-x[0:n-a])
  return c*dx
```
This is O(active) Python dot, each C. Good. For active 30, n=1024 =>30k, fast. For gradient active, same loops add slices.

`select_active(c,n,top_k,tol_rel=1e-5,tol_abs=1e-6)`:
```
M=c.max(); tol=max(tol_abs, tol_rel*M)
mask=c>=M-tol
idx=np.where(mask)[0]
if len(idx)>top_k:
  order=np.argsort(c[idx])
  idx=idx[order[-top_k:]]
else:
  # ensure at least top_min? If len<5, take top min(20,n-1) by c
  if len(idx)<min(10,top_k): idx=np.argsort(c)[-min(top_k, len(c))][:]
return np.asarray(idx)
```

`upsample`:
```
def upsample(h,n,nt):
 if n==nt return h
 x0=np.linspace(0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
 x1=np.linspace(0,2.0,nt,endpoint=False)
 h1=np.interp(x1,x0,h) # maybe linear
 return project_feasible(h1,nt)
```
Linear interpolation may make sum not exact; project.

Let's write active functions with weights:

def active_weights_from_c(c, alpha):
 m=c.max(); sh=alpha*(c-m); mx=sh.max()
 w=np.exp(sh-mx); w/=w.sum(); return w, m
```
In objective, after weights recompute? To avoid duplicated code, but fine.

`active_obj(x,n,active,alpha,mu)`:

c=active_c_vec(x,n,active)
m=c.max()
if len(c)==1: logterm=0.0
else:
 sh=alpha*(c-m); mx=sh.max()
 logterm=mx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx)))
val=m+logterm/alpha
sm=x.sum(); err=sm-n/2.0
if mu: val += mu*err*err
return float(val)
```

`run_active_opt` with `mu`:
```
def run_active_opt(h,n,deadline,top_k=20,alphas=None,mu=0.05,max_rounds=60):
 if alphas is None: alphas=(100,1000,10000,50000)
 hcur=project_feasible(h,n); cc=c5_direct(hcur,n)
 hbest=hcur.copy(); cbest=cc
 bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
 no_improve=0
 for r in range(max_rounds):
   if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
   round_improved=False
   for alpha in alphas:
     if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
     c=c_values(hcur,n); M=c.max()
     active=select_active(c,n,top_k,1e-5)
     if active is None or len(active)==0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
     rem=deadline - time.time()
     if rem < 3: break
     rate = 250.0 if n <= 4096 else 50.0? Since active only; choose cap.
     maxit = int(min(1500 if n>700 else 2500, max(200, rem* (100 if n>700 else 200))))
     # Actually rem*rate too high. Use cap.
     maxit = min(maxit, 2000 if n<=600 else 1000)
     try:
       res=minimize(active_obj, hcur, jac=active_grad, args=(n,active,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-20,'gtol':1e-14,'maxcor':50,'maxls':40})
       hnew=project_feasible(res.x,n); cnew=c5_direct(hnew,n)
       if cnew < cc - 1e-14:
          hcur=hnew; cc=cnew
          if cnew < cbest - 1e-14:
             hbest=hnew.copy(); cbest=cnew; no_improve=0; round_improved=True
          else: no_improve += 0? not global
       elif cnew <= cc + 1e-7:
          hcur=hnew; cc=cnew
          if cnew < cbest: ...
       else:
          no_improve += 1
     except Exception:
       no_improve += 1
   if not round_improved:
      no_improve += 1
   else:
      no_improve=0
   if no_improve >= 8: break
 return hbest, cbest
```

 R=60 if n>700 else 70

 # cross
 for ii in low:
   i=int(ii)
   for jj in high:
     j=int(jj)
     if i!=j:
       a,b=(i,j) if i<j else (j,i)
       pairs.add((a,b))
 # local radius
 rad = 12 if n<=700 else 8
 for i in range(0,n,1): # all
   for d in range(1,rad+1):
      j=i+d
      if j<n and abs(h[i]-h[j])>1e-3:
        pairs.add((i,j))
 # random
 rpairs=2000
 for _ in range(rpairs):
   i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i!=j: ...
 # score sort
 lst=[]
 for a,b in pairs:
    if a==b: continue
    if abs(h[a]-h[b]) < 1e-4: continue
    score=abs(g[a]-g[b]) + 0.2*abs(h[a]-h[b])
    lst.append((score,a,b))
 lst.sort(reverse=True)
 maxp=500? maybe 5000
 return [ (a,b) for s,a,b in lst[:maxp] ]
```

`c5_direct`:
```
def c5_direct(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')*(2.0/n)))
```

`c_values` same. Use `n` maybe int.

`active_c_vec`:
```
def active_c_vec(x,n,active):
    dx=2.0/n
    m=len(active)
    c=np.empty(m,float)
    for t in range(m):
       k=active[t]-(n-1)
       if k>=0:
          nv=n-k
          c[t]=np.dot(x[0:nv], 1.0 - x[k:n])*dx
       else:
          a=-k
          nv=n-a
          c[t]=np.dot(x[a:n], 1.0 - x[0:nv])*dx
    return c
```
For k=0, nv=n, dot(x,1-x). Good.

`grad_from_active`:
```
def grad_from_active(x,n,active,c, alpha, mu=0.0):
    m=c.max()
    if len(c)>1:
       sh=alpha*(c-m); mx=sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh-mx); w/=w.sum()
    else: w=np.ones(1,float)
    dx=2.0/n; grad=np.zeros(n,float)
    for wi,idx in zip(w,active):
       if wi <= 1e-16: continue
       k=int(idx)-(n-1); wm=wi*dx
       if k>=0:
          nv=n-k
          if nv>0:
             grad[0:nv] += wm*(1.0 - x[k:n])
             grad[k:n] += -wm*x[0:nv]
       else:
          a=-k; nv=n-a
          if nv>0:
             grad[a:n] += wm*(1.0 - x[0:nv])
             grad[0:nv] += -wm*x[a:n]
    if mu:
       err=x.sum()-n/2.0
       if abs(err)>1e-14: grad += 2.0*mu*err
    return grad
```
For negative k, derivative formula earlier: grad[a:n] += wm*(1-x[0:nv]) (i=j term), grad[0:nv] += -wm*x[a:n]. Check: k=-a, c=∑_{i=a}^{n-1} h_i (1-h_{i-a}). Deriv wrt h_j:
 i=j: if j>=a -> 1-h_{j-a}; that's grad[a:n] += 1-x[0:nv].
 i-a=j: if j+a<n -> -h_{j+a}; grad[0:nv] += -x[a:n]. Good.
For k=0, first part grad[0:n]+=1-x; second part grad[0:n]+=-x ->1-2x. Good.

def active_obj(x,n,active,alpha,mu):
 c=active_c_vec(x,n,active)
...
```

`select_active`: ensure returns np.array ints.
```
def select_active(c,n,top_k,tol_rel=1e-5):
 M=float(np.max(c))
 tol=max(1e-9, tol_rel*M)
 idx=np.where(c >= M - tol)[0]
 if len(idx)>top_k:
    order=np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]
    idx=idx[order[:top_k]]
 else:
    if len(idx) < max(3, min(10, top_k)):
       top=min(top_k,len(c))
       idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top]
 return idx
```

 else: rate, cap=50, 800
 mi=int(remaining*rate)
 return max(300, min(cap, mi))
```

```
def run_active_opt(h,n,deadline,top_k=20,alphas=None,mu=0.05,max_rounds=60):
 if len(h)!=n: h=np.resize(h,n)
 hcur=project_feasible(h,n)
 cc=float(c5_direct(hcur,n))
 hbest=hcur.copy(); cbest=cc
 if alphas is None: alphas=(100.0,1000.0,10000.0,50000.0)
 bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for _ in range(n)]? No lambda? list comp okay? Lambda not closure? List comprehension not closure? It's fine but to be safe: `bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n`.
 stagnant=0
 for r in range(max_rounds):
   if time.time() > deadline - 3: break
   for alpha in alphas:
     if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
     remaining=deadline-time.time()
     if remaining < 12: break
     c=c_values(hcur,n)
     active=select_active(c,n,top_k,1e-5)
     if len(active)==0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))],dtype=int)
     maxit=get_maxiter(n,remaining)
     if maxit <=0: break
     try:
       res=minimize(active_obj,hcur,jac=active_grad,args=(n,active,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-20,'gtol':1e-14,'maxcor':50,'maxls':30})
       hnew=project_feasible(res.x,n)
       cnew=float(c5_direct(hnew,n))
     except Exception:
       stagnant += 1; continue
     if np.isfinite(cnew):
       if cnew < cbest - 1e-13:
          hbest=hnew.copy(); cbest=cnew; stagnant=0
       else:
          stagnant += 1
       if cnew < cc - 1e-14:
          hcur=hnew.copy(); cc=cnew
       elif cnew <= cc + 1e-7:
          hcur=hnew.copy(); cc=cnew
       else:
          # keep current, maybe no update
          pass
     if stagnant > 18:
        break
   if stagnant > 18: break
 if cc < cbest: cbest=cc; hbest=hcur.copy() # current may equal
 return hbest,cbest
```
If no best improvement but current improved not best? impossible; cbest initial. Good.
Stagnant increments even if current improves but not best. Could break too early; set 30. Use 30.

`sym_active_grad`:

def sym_active_grad(half,n,active,alpha,mu):
 full=mirror_full(half,n)
 c=active_c_vec(full,n,active)
 fullgrad=grad_from_active(full,n,active,c,alpha,0.0)
 m=len(half)
 grad=fullgrad[:m] + fullgrad[n-1:m-1:-1]
 if mu:
   err=2.0*half.sum() - n/2.0
   grad += 4.0*mu*err
 return grad
```
Because penalty derivative: err=2sum-S, d(err²)/dhalf=2err*2=4err. Good.

`make_binary_start(h,n)`:
```
def make_binary_start(h,n):
 idx=np.argsort(-h)
 b=np.zeros(n,float)
 if n%2==0:
    cnt=n//2
    b[idx[:cnt]]=1.0
    rem=n/2.0 - cnt
 else:
    cnt=(n-1)//2
    b[idx[:cnt]]=1.0
    rem=n/2.0 - cnt  # 0.5
 if abs(rem)>1e-12 and cnt<n:
    b[idx[cnt]] += rem
 return project_feasible(b,n)
```
For odd, rem .5.

def pair_generate(g,h,n,seed):
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 R = 70 if n <= 700 else 50
 order=np.argsort(g)
 low=order[:R]
 high=order[-R:][::-1]
 pairs=set()
 for i in low:
   i=int(i)
   for j in high:
     j=int(j)
     if i!=j:
       if i<j: pairs.add((i,j))
       else: pairs.add((j,i))
 nrand=1200
 for _ in range(nrand):
   i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
   if i!=j:
      if i<j: pairs.add((i,j))
      else: pairs.add((j,i))
 # optionally local if pairs small? add local radius 4
 rad=4
 for i in range(0,n,2): # every other to reduce
   for d in range(1,rad+1):
      j=i+d
      if j<n and abs(h[i]-h[j])>1e-3:
         pairs.add((i,j))
 max_pairs=2500
 lst=[]
 for a,b in pairs:
   if a==b: continue
   if abs(h[a]-h[b]) < 1e-4: continue
   score=abs(g[a]-g[b]) + 0.1*abs(h[a]-h[b])
   lst.append((score,a,b))
 lst.sort(key=lambda x:x[0], reverse=True) # lambda? no lambda? The rule no closures or lambdas. Use `(lst, reverse=True)`? Tuple list sortable by default first element reverse. Since items are (score,a,b), `lst.sort(reverse=True)` sorts by score desc. Good no lambda.
 if len(lst)>max_pairs: lst=lst[:max_pairs]
 return [(a,b) for item in lst] # list comp okay not closure.
```

`pair_exact_d`:
```
def pair_exact_d(h,pair,active,c_active,n):
 i,j=pair
 dx=2.0/n
 dlo=max(-h[i], h[i]-1.0? wait h[i]-1 yes) ...
```
Double-check dlo formula:
h_i new = h_i + d >=0 => d>= -h_i
h_i new <=1 => d<=1-h_i
h_j new = h_j - d >=0 => d <= h_j
h_j new <=1 => -d <=1-h_j => d >= h_j -1
So dlo=max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0), dhi=min(1-h[i], h[j]). Correct.
If dhi < dlo +1e-14 return 0.0, inf.

```

 ai=0.0
 if 0 <= i+k < n: ai += 1.0 - h[i+k]

 aj=0.0

```

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _project_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x[~np.isfinite(x)] = 0.5
    target = float(target)
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    if target >= x.size:
        return np.ones_like(x)

    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        y = np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(y.sum())
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    return np.clip(x + lam, 0.0, 1.0)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size < n:
            hp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            hp[:h.size] = h
            h = hp
        else:
            h = h[:n]
    return _project_sum(h, 0.5 * n)


def c5_direct(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / n)))


def c_values(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / n)


def active_c_vec(x, n, active):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    out = np.empty(len(active), dtype=float)
    for t, idx in enumerate(active):
        k = int(idx) - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            if nv <= 0:
                out[t] = 0.0
            else:
                out[t] = float(np.dot(x[0:nv], 1.0 - x[k:n]) * dx)
        else:
            a = -k
            nv = n - a
            if nv <= 0:
                out[t] = 0.0
            else:
                out[t] = float(np.dot(x[a:n], 1.0 - x[0:nv]) * dx)
    return out


def select_active(c, n, top_k, tol_rel=1e-5):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.array([0], dtype=int)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    tol = max(1e-10, tol_rel * abs(M))
    idx = np.where(c >= M - tol)[0]
    if len(idx) == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    if len(idx) > top_k:
        order = np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]
        idx = idx[order[:top_k]]
    else:
        need = max(5, min(10, top_k))
        if len(idx) < need:
            take = min(top_k, len(c))
            idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:take]
    return np.asarray(idx, dtype=int)


def grad_from_active(x, n, active, c, alpha, mu=0.0):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if c.size > 1:
        sh = alpha * (c - m)
        mx = float(np.max(sh))
        w = np.exp(sh - mx)
        w = w / float(w.sum())
    else:
        w = np.ones(1, dtype=float)

    dx = 2.0 / n
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for wi, idx in zip(w, active):
        if wi <= 1e-16:
            continue
        k = int(idx) - (n - 1)
        wm = float(wi) * dx
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            if nv > 0:
                g[0:nv] += wm * (1.0 - x[k:n])
                g[k:n] += -wm * x[0:nv]
        else:
            a = -k
            nv = n - a
            if nv > 0:
                g[a:n] += wm * (1.0 - x[0:nv])
                g[0:nv] += -wm * x[a:n]

    if mu != 0.0:
        err = float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n
        if abs(err) > 1e-14:
            g += 2.0 * mu * err
    return g


def active_obj(x, n, active, alpha, mu):
    c = active_c_vec(x, n, active)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e100
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if c.size > 1:
        sh = alpha * (c - m)
        mx = float(np.max(sh))
        logsum = mx + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh - mx))))
    else:
        logsum = 0.0
    val = m + logsum / alpha
    if mu != 0.0:
        err = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * n
        val += mu * err * err
    return float(val)


def active_grad(x, n, active, alpha, mu):
    c = active_c_vec(x, n, active)
    return grad_from_active(x, n, active, c, alpha, mu)


def get_maxiter(n, remaining):
    if remaining <= 0:
        return 0
    if n <= 512:
        rate = 240.0
        cap = 2400
    elif n <= 1024:
        rate = 110.0
        cap = 1200
    else:
        rate = 60.0
        cap = 800
    mi = int(remaining * rate)
    return max(250, min(int(cap), mi))


def run_active_opt(h, n, deadline, top_k=20, alphas=None, mu=0.05, max_rounds=60):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
    hcur = project_feasible(h, n)
    ccur = c5_direct(hcur, n)
    hbest = hcur.copy()
    cbest = ccur

    if alphas is None:
        alphas = (100.0, 1000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    stagnant = 0

    for _ in range(max_rounds):
        if time.time() > deadline - 4:
            break
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 10:
                break
            rem = deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 12:
                break
            c = c_values(hcur, n)
            active = select_active(c, n, top_k, 1e-5)
            if len(active) == 0:
                active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
            maxit = get_maxiter(n, rem)
            if maxit <= 0:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    active_obj,
                    hcur,
                    jac=active_grad,
                    args=(n, active, float(alpha), float(mu)),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": maxit,
                        "ftol": 1e-20,
                        "gtol": 1e-14,
                        "maxcor": 50,
                        "maxls": 30,
                    },
                )
                hnew = project_feasible(res.x, n)
                cnew = c5_direct(hnew, n)
            except Exception:
                stagnant += 1
                if stagnant > 30:
                    break
                continue

            if np.isfinite(cnew):
                if cnew < cbest - 1e-13:
                    hbest = hnew.copy()
                    cbest = cnew
                    stagnant = 0
                else:
                    stagnant += 1

                if cnew < ccur - 1e-14:
                    hcur = hnew.copy()
                    ccur = cnew
                elif cnew <= ccur + 1e-7:
                    hcur = hnew.copy()
                    ccur = cnew
            else:
                stagnant += 1

            if stagnant > 30:
                break
        if stagnant > 30:
            break

    if ccur < cbest:
        cbest = ccur
        hbest = hcur.copy()
    return hbest, cbest


def upsample(h, n, nt):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n == nt:
        return project_feasible(h, nt)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n), endpoint=False)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(nt), endpoint=False)
    h1 = np.interp(x1, x0, h)
    return project_feasible(h1, nt)


def symmetrize_full(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])


def mirror_full(half, n):
    m = n // 2
    full = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    full[:m] = half
    full[m:] = half[::-1]
    return full


def run_sym_active_opt(h, n_best, n_sym, deadline, top_k=18, alphas=None, mu=0.05, max_rounds=40):
    if n_sym % 2 != 0:
        n_sym += 1
    m = n_sym // 2
    target = 0.25 * n_sym

    hup = upsample(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), int(n_best), n_sym)
    hfull = symmetrize_full(hup, n_sym)
    hfull = project_feasible(hfull, n_sym)
    half = project_feasible(hfull[:m], 0.5 * n_sym)[:m]
    half = _project_sum(half, target)

    full = mirror_full(half, n_sym)
    ccur = c5_direct(full, n_sym)
    hbest_half = half.copy()
    cbest = ccur

    if alphas is None:
        alphas = (100.0, 1000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0)

    half_obj = half
    def sym_obj(x, n, active, alpha, mu_val):
        full = mirror_full(x, n)
        c = active_c_vec(full, n, active)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            return 1e100
        mxv = float(np.max(c))
        if c.size > 1:
            sh = alpha * (c - mxv)
            sx = float(np.max(sh))
            logsum = sx + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh - sx))))
        else:
            logsum = 0.0
        val = mxv + logsum / alpha
        if mu_val != 0.0:
            err = 2.0 * float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * n
            val += mu_val * err * err
        return float(val)

    def sym_grad(x, n, active, alpha, mu_val):
        full = mirror_full(x, n)
        c = active_c_vec(full, n, active)
        fg = grad_from_active(full, n, active, c, alpha, 0.0)
        gg = fg[:m] + fg[n - 1:m - 1:-1]
        if mu_val != 0.0:
            err = 2.0 * float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * n
            gg += 4.0 * mu_val * err
        return gg

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
    stagnant = 0

    for _ in range(max_rounds):
        if time.time() > deadline - 4:
            break
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 10:
                break
            rem = deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 12:
                break
            full = mirror_full(half, n_sym)
            c = c_values(full, n_sym)
            active = select_active(c, n_sym, top_k, 1e-5)
            if len(active) == 0:
                active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
            maxit = get_maxiter(n_sym, rem)
            if maxit <= 0:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    sym_obj,
                    half,
                    jac=sym_grad,
                    args=(n_sym, active, float(alpha), float(mu)),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": maxit,
                        "ftol": 1e-20,
                        "gtol": 1e-14,
                        "maxcor": 50,
                        "maxls": 30,
                    },
                )
                hnew = _project_sum(res.x, target)
                fnew = mirror_full(hnew, n_sym)
                cnew = c5_direct(fnew, n_sym)
            except Exception:
                stagnant += 1
                if stagnant > 25:
                    break
                continue

            if np.isfinite(cnew):
                if cnew < cbest - 1e-13:
                    hbest_half = hnew.copy()
                    cbest = cnew
                    stagnant = 0
                else:
                    stagnant += 1

                if cnew < ccur - 1e-14:
                    half = hnew.copy()
                    ccur = cnew
                elif cnew <= ccur + 1e-7:
                    half = hnew.copy()
                    ccur = cnew
            else:
                stagnant += 1

            if stagnant > 25:
                break
        if stagnant > 25:
            break

    if ccur < cbest:
        cbest = ccur
        hbest_half = half.copy()
    full = mirror_full(_project_sum(hbest_half, target), n_sym)
    cfull = c5_direct(full, n_sym)
    return full, cfull


def make_binary_start(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argsort(-h)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        cnt = n // 2
        b[idx[:cnt]] = 1.0
        rem = 0.5 * n - float(cnt)
    else:
        cnt = (n - 1) // 2
        b[idx[:cnt]] = 1.0
        rem = 0.5 * n - float(cnt)
    if abs(rem) > 1e-12 and cnt < n:
        b[idx[cnt]] += rem
    return project_feasible(b, n)


def pair_generate(g, h, n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)

    R = 70 if n <= 700 else 50
    order = np.argsort(g)
    low = order[:R]
    high = order[-R:][::-1]

    pairs = set()
    for ii in low:
        i = int(ii)
        for jj in high:
            j = int(jj)
            if i != j:
                if i < j:
                    pairs.add((i, j))
                else:
                    pairs.add((j, i))

    nrand = 1200
    for _ in range(nrand):
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i != j:
            if i < j:
                pairs.add((i, j))
            else:
                pairs.add((j, i))

    rad = 4
    step = 2 if n > 800 else 1
    for i in range(0, n, step):
        for d in range(1, rad + 1):
            j = i + d
            if j < n and abs(h[i] - h[j]) > 1e-3:
                pairs.add((i, j))

    lst = []
    for a, b in pairs:
        if a == b:
            continue
        if abs(h[a] - h[b]) < 1e-4:
            continue
        score = abs(g[a] - g[b]) + 0.1 * abs(h[a] - h[b])
        lst.append((score, a, b))
    lst.sort(reverse=True)

    max_pairs = 2500
    res = []
    for item in lst:
        if len(res) >= max_pairs:
            break
        res.append((item[1], item[2]))
    return res


def pair_exact_d(h, pair, active, c_active, n):
    i, j = pair
    dx = 2.0 / n

    dlo = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
    dhi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
    if dhi < dlo + 1e-14:
        return 0.0, float("inf")

    m = len(active)
    base = c_active
    aa = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
    bb = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
    diff = i - j

    for t, idx in enumerate(active):
        k = int(idx) - (n - 1)

        ai = 0.0
        if 0 <= i + k < n:
            ai += 1.0 - h[i + k]
        if 0 <= i - k < n:
            ai -= h[i - k]

        aj = 0.0
        if 0 <= j + k < n:
            aj += 1.0 - h[j + k]
        if 0 <= j - k < n:
            aj -= h[j - k]

        aa[t] = (ai - aj) * dx
        if k == 0:
            bb[t] = -2.0 * dx
        elif diff != 0 and abs(diff) == abs(k):
            bb[t] = 1.0 * dx
        else:
            bb[t] = 0.0

    cands = [dlo, dhi]
    if dlo < -1e-12 and dhi > 1e-12:
        cands.append(0.0)

    for t in range(m):
        if abs(bb[t]) > 1e-15:
            dv = -aa[t] / (2.0 * bb[t])
            if dlo - 1e-12 <= dv <= dhi + 1e-12:
                cands.append(dv)

    for q in range(1, 17):
        d = dlo + q * (dhi - dlo) / 16.0
        cands.append(d)

    for p in range(m):
        for q in range(p + 1, m):
            A = bb[p] - bb[q]
            B = aa[p] - aa[q]
            C = base[p] - base[q]
            if abs(A) > 1e-15:
                disc = B * B - 4.0 * A * C
                if disc >= -1e-14:
                    if disc < 0.0:
                        disc = 0.0
                    s = float(np.sqrt(disc))
                    r1 = (-B + s) / (2.0 * A)
                    r2 = (-B - s) / (2.0 * A)
                    if dlo - 1e-12 <= r1 <= dhi + 1e-12:
                        cands.append(r1)
                    if dlo - 1e-12 <= r2 <= dhi + 1e-12:
                        cands.append(r2)
            else:
                if abs(B) > 1e-15:
                    r = -C / B
                    if dlo - 1e-12 <= r <= dhi + 1e-12:
                        cands.append(r)

    best_val = float("inf")
    best_d = 0.0
    for d in cands:
        if not np.isfinite(d):
            continue
        if d < dlo:
            d = dlo
        elif d > dhi:
            d = dhi
        vals = base + aa * d + bb * d * d
        v = float(np.max(vals))
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            continue
        if v < best_val - 1e-15:
            best_val = v
            best_d = d
        elif abs(v - best_val) < 1e-12 and abs(d) < abs(best_d):
            best_d = d
    return best_d, best_val


def pair_line_search(h, n, deadline, top_k=18, max_exact_pairs=16, max_trials_per_iter=45, max_iters=300):
    hbest = project_feasible(h, n)
    cbest = c5_direct(hbest, n)
    seed = 123
    stagnant = 0

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.time() > deadline - 4:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 5:
            break

        c = c_values(hbest, n)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        active = select_active(c, n, top_k, 1e-5)
        if len(active) == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
        c_active = c[active]

        g = grad_from_active(hbest, n, active, c_active, 1000.0, 0.0)
        pairs = pair_generate(g, hbest, n, seed)
        seed += 1
        if not pairs:
            break

        tested = 0
        best_try_c = cbest
        best_try_h = None

        for pair in pairs[:max_exact_pairs]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 6:
                break
            if tested >= max_trials_per_iter:
                break
            for frac in (1.0, 0.6, 0.3):
                if tested >= max_trials_per_iter:
                    break
                d, aval = pair_exact_d(hbest, pair, active, c_active, n)
                if abs(d) < 1e-13:
                    break
                if not (aval < M - 1e-10):
                    break
                dd = d * frac
                if abs(dd) < 1e-13:
                    continue
                i, j = pair
                ht = hbest.copy()
                ht[i] += dd
                ht[j] -= dd
                ht = np.clip(ht, 0.0, 1.0)
                ht = project_feasible(ht, n)
                ct = c5_direct(ht, n)
                tested += 1
                if np.isfinite(ct) and ct < best_try_c:
                    best_try_c = ct
                    best_try_h = ht.copy()

        if best_try_h is not None and best_try_c < cbest - 1e-14:
            hbest = best_try_h
            cbest = best_try_c
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1

        if stagnant > 8:
            break

    return project_feasible(hbest, n), c5_direct(hbest, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    B = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + B - 10.0

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 512
        rs = np.random.RandomState(seed)
        h0 = (rs.rand(n0) < 0.5).astype(float)

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = c5_direct(h0, n0)
    best_n = int(n0)

    end1 = t0 + 0.22 * B
    if time.time() < end1 - 10:
        h1, c1 = run_active_opt(
            best_h, best_n, end1,
            top_k=20,
            alphas=(50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 50000.0),
            mu=0.05,
        )
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1
            best_n = len(h1)

    if time.time() < t0 + 0.55 * B - 20 and best_n < 1024:
        n_hi = 1024
        h_hi = upsample(best_h, best_n, n_hi)
        h2, c2 = run_active_opt(
            h_hi, n_hi, t0 + 0.55 * B,
            top_k=20,
            alphas=(50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 50000.0),
            mu=0.05,
        )
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2
            best_n = len(h2)

    end3 = t0 + 0.68 * B
    if time.time() < end3 - 25:
        hb = make_binary_start(best_h, best_n)
        cb = c5_direct(hb, best_n)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_c = cb
            best_h = hb
        h3, c3 = run_active_opt(
            hb, best_n, end3,
            top_k=20,
            alphas=(100.0, 1000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0),
            mu=0.05,
            max_rounds=25,
        )
        if c3 < best_c:
            best_c = c3
            best_h = h3
            best_n = len(h3)

    end4 = t0 + 0.78 * B
    if time.time() < end4 - 30:
        n_sym = 1024 if best_n >= 512 else 512
        h4, c4 = run_sym_active_opt(
            best_h, best_n, n_sym, end4,
            top_k=18,
            alphas=(100.0, 1000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0),
            mu=0.05,
            max_rounds=25,
        )
        if c4 < best_c:
            best_c = c4
            best_h = h4
            best_n = len(h4)

    end5 = deadline - 0.08 * B
    if time.time() < end5 - 30:
        h5, c5 = pair_line_search(
            best_h, best_n, end5,
            top_k=18,
            max_exact_pairs=16,
            max_trials_per_iter=45,
            max_iters=300,
        )
        if c5 < best_c:
            best_c = c5
            best_h = h5
            best_n = len(h5)

    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
        h6, c6 = run_active_opt(
            best_h, best_n, deadline - 4,
            top_k=12,
            alphas=(50000.0, 200000.0),
            mu=0.05,
            max_rounds=12,
        )
        if c6 < best_c:
            best_c = c6
            best_h = h6
            best_n = len(h6)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = c5_direct(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_n)
```
